- **gno-build**: Use when a new realm or `/p/` package is wanted, when a function is added to or changed in one, when a Gno project is scaffolded, or when `.gno` code is deployed or published to a chain — even when only the on-chain behavior is described ("make a realm that stores notes", "deploy a counter I can bump", "add a withdraw function") and "build" is never said. Entered BEFORE you write any `.gno` source, since Gno's semantics and API differ from Go's and authoring from memory ships a realm that works by luck. Reasoning about existing code stays with gno, security audits with gno-audit, failed trans… ([gnoverse/gno-mcp](https://github.com/gnoverse/gno-mcp/tree/HEAD/skills/gno-build) · ★3)
- **gno-debug**: Use when a gno.land transaction failed, when a gnomcp write tool returned an error (insufficient_funds, authentication_required, scope_mismatch, invalid sequence, panic, out of gas), when a gno_call/gno_run/gno_addpkg result looks wrong, or when a chain error message is pasted — even if "debug" is never said. Entered BEFORE you retry, re-sign or re-broadcast anything, since a blind retry costs the fee again and hides the cause. ([gnoverse/gno-mcp](https://github.com/gnoverse/gno-mcp/tree/HEAD/skills/gno-debug) · ★3)
- **gno-onboard**: Use when someone asks "what is gno", "how do I start with gno.land", "explain gno to me", says they are new to Gno or coming from another stack (Solidity, Solana, web2, anything), or asks beginner questions about realms, gnokey, mainnet, or the testnet — even without the word "onboard". Entered at the start of the conversation, BEFORE you explain anything or generate a key, since the first step is finding out what they already know. ([gnoverse/gno-mcp](https://github.com/gnoverse/gno-mcp/tree/HEAD/skills/gno-onboard) · ★3)

- **gnomorphogenetic-hermeneutics**: WHAT: a hermeneutics (interpretive method) and a forge for NAMING an unlabeled recursive / self-referential / holographic pattern — stack cross-domain analogies, triangulate the invariant, coin a NEW word by mixing morphemes, and install it as a semantic-attractor BLOCK (an 'eigenword block') that directs an agent's recursive awareness to a fixed point instead of letting it regress. WHEN: when a pattern you keep circling has no name of its own (every label is borrowed or esoteric), when meta-awareness won't terminate, when you need to coin a term, or when you need to write a flourish block fo… ([sancovp/doc-mirror](https://github.com/sancovp/doc-mirror/tree/HEAD/skills/doc-mirror-prompts/resources/prompts/authoring/gnomorphogenetic-hermeneutics))
- **gnose**: Reveal how an LLM interprets a prompt before or after running it. Use when the user asks how their prompt/SKILL/system prompt will be read or "understood", why an output didn't match what they wanted, to diagnose a prompt against a bad output, or invokes "gnose" by name. Covers: interpreting short utterances, predicting which parts of a long prompt get weighted or ignored, diagnosing prompt-vs-output divergence, and reshaping prompts to match corrected intent. Not for general prompt-writing requests that don't involve inspecting interpretation. ([inthepond/inthepond-skills](https://github.com/inthepond/inthepond-skills/tree/HEAD/gnose))

- **gnoto-lite**: GNOTO ノート（.gnoto）を Claude の中で作る・直す・見る。ノーマルとスライドの 2 つだけ。「ノートを作って」「GNOTO のスライドにして」「このノートに条文を入れて」「判例を入れて」「.gnoto を直して」「プレビューを見せて」と頼まれたとき、法律モード（条文・判例・引用の取り込み）を使うときに使う。条文・判例はコネクタ（なければ e-Gov・裁判所サイト）から取り、出典つきで書く。laba の出力先を GNOTO にしたいときにも使う。 ([Yoshiaki-Takashita/Laba-plugin](https://github.com/Yoshiaki-Takashita/Laba-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/gnoto-lite) · ★1)
- **gnps-api-npclassifier-classification-retrieval**: Use when you have SMILES strings for natural products (e.g. from MIBiG json entries) and need biosynthetically-informed chemical class terms (pathway/class/subclass) to match against BGC class ontologies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-api-npclassifier-classification-retrieval))
- **gnps-data-format-conversion-and-mapping**: Use when you have GNPS molecular networking output (from GNPS1 at https://gnps.ucsd.edu or GNPS2 at https://gnps2.org) that must be integrated with antiSMASH BGC data and MIBiG metadata for natural product mining. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/gnps-data-format-conversion-and-mapping) · 2 copies)
- **gnps-data-schema-validation**: Use when after extracting a GNPS molecular networking job archive using GNPSExtractor, before calling npl.load_data(). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/gnps-data-schema-validation) · 2 copies)
- **gnps-dataset-acquisition-and-curation**: Use when reproducing or extending the MetaMiner peptidogenomics pipeline and you need to identify which GNPS MSVxxxxxx accessions have paired genomic/metagenomic data (e.g. MSV000079506, MSV000079622, MSV000078839, MSV000078604, MSV000078552, MSV000080102) and stage them for RiPP search. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gnps-dataset-acquisition-and-curation))
- **gnps-dataset-curation**: Use when MS/MS spectra loaded from MGF, mzML, mzXML, or msp files carry inconsistent or missing metadata (compound name, InChI, SMILES, InChIKey, ionmode, adduct, charge) and must be cleaned before intensity normalization or similarity scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-dataset-curation))
- **gnps-dereplication-workflow-execution**: Use when mzML LC-MS/MS runs have been processed by Optimus and detected features need putative compound identities via MS/MS spectral library matching at GNPS, to be attached by mz-RT matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-dereplication-workflow-execution))
- **gnps-drug-library-spectral-matching**: Use when untargeted LC-MS/MS metabolomics data (MZmine-processed features with MS/MS spectra) must be screened for drugs, metabolites, and analogs to infer drug exposures empirically, e.g. matching fecal-incubation analogs to community metabolism. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-drug-library-spectral-matching))
- **gnps-fbmn-export-file-preparation**: Use when a processed LC-MS/MS feature map (consensusXML after feature detection, alignment, adduct annotation, and feature linking) must be exported to run a GNPS FBMN or IIMN job downstream. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-fbmn-export-file-preparation))
- **gnps-fbmn-job-submission**: Use when an aligned MS1 feature quantification table with consensus MS2 spectra and IIN edge files (.csv) is ready and an ion identity molecular network must be generated on the GNPS web server. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-fbmn-job-submission))
- **gnps-fbmn-job-submission-and-network-import**: Use when an LC-MS/MS dataset has already been processed to a feature quantification table (row ID, row m/z, row retention time, peak area) plus a .MGF MS/MS spectral summary, and the goal is a GNPS FBMN job and a Cytoscape network in which bioactive nodes can be flagged. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-fbmn-job-submission-and-network-import))
- **gnps-fbmn-output-processing**: Use when the inputs are an MZmine join-aligner output (aligned feature intensity CSV) plus its GNPS FBMN-exported .mgf spectra file, and the goal is an RT-agnostic sample-by-feature presence/absence MEMO matrix rather than an intensity-based comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-fbmn-output-processing))
- **gnps-feature-based-molecular-networking-rerun**: Use when a downstream prioritization step (e.g. Inventa FC score) needs a regenerated GNPS FBMN job — node/edge/IIN counts and cleaned spectral annotations — from a MZmine quant table and metadata file for an untargeted LC-HRMS/MS extract set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-feature-based-molecular-networking-rerun))
- **gnps-feature-network-import-and-scoring**: Use when starting from a GNPS feature network (.graphml) plus bioassay CSVs (or MZmine 2 peak lists / mzML centroids) and you need Activity Scores, Cluster Scores, and bioactive communities to prioritize metabolites for isolation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-feature-network-import-and-scoring))
- **gnps-graphml-feature-import**: Use when the input mass spectrometry data is a GNPS network file (GNPS.graphml) and MS features must be imported and basketed as the first npanalyst CLI step before activity-score computation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-graphml-feature-import))
- **gnps-job-output-parsing**: Use when a practitioner has a GNPS classical or feature-based molecular networking job and needs its graphml/tsv/clustered-MGF outputs parsed and matched to downstream annotation files (MS2LDA summaries, ClassyFire/NAP/DEREPLICATOR results) before running MolNetEnhancer. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-job-output-parsing))
- **gnps-library-format-assembly**: Use when you have extracted MS1 and MS2 scans (in mzML/mzXML format) from raw chromatogram files and possess user-provided metadata (retention time, m/z, compound name, molecular weight, annotation fields) that must be combined into a single structured library entry suitable for spectral library. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/gnps-library-format-assembly) · 2 copies)
- **gnps-library-match-filtering**: Use when an aligned feature table with MS2 spectra (.mgf) and IIN edges is available and high-confidence GNPS library annotations must be identified and propagated to co-eluting ion species (adducts, multimers, in-source fragments) of the same compound. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-library-match-filtering))
- **gnps-library-search-execution**: Use when an mzmine (&gt;2.53) FBMN feature table (CSV) and MGF MS/MS spectra need GNPS library annotations, and the goal is to compare strict single-query vs iterative weighted analog search InChI return rates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-library-search-execution))
- **gnps-library-search-results-retrieval**: Use when the goal is a repository-scale chemical annotation table across public tandem MS files, e.g. reproducing the ReDU compound-by-file GNPS library search matrix or updating ReDU's identification database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-library-search-results-retrieval))
- **gnps-library-spectrum-preprocessing**: Use when preparing a downloaded GNPS MGF library (e.g. ALL_GNPS.mgf) as training data or as a cleaned search library for MS2Deepscore/Spec2Vec/MS2Query, so that spectra are uniform and noise-free. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-library-spectrum-preprocessing))
- **gnps-library-spectrum-processing**: Use when a public GNPS/MassBank/MoNA .msp library needs reproducible cleaning before library matching or machine-learning training: metadata harmonization, adduct/parent-mass repair, annotation validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-library-spectrum-processing))
- **gnps-massive-dataset-execution**: Use when a GC-MS dataset deposited on GNPS/MassIVE (an MSV accession) must be processed into deconvoluted compound fragmentation spectra via the MSHub/GNPS workflow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-massive-dataset-execution))
- **gnps-massive-dataset-retrieval-and-reprocessing**: Use when reproducing or extending analyses from a publicly deposited untargeted metabolomics dataset (e.g. peak-area feature tables plus sample metadata hosted on GNPS/MassIVE or Qiita) instead of acquiring new instrument data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-massive-dataset-retrieval-and-reprocessing))
- **gnps-masst-repository-mining**: Use when KGMN (or similar) has produced putatively annotated unknown metabolites with precursor m/z and MS/MS spectra and you need to test whether they recur in public metabolomics repositories (GNPS/MassIVE) as an orthogonal validation signal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-masst-repository-mining))
- **gnps-matching-peak-scoring**: Use when dereplicating preprocessed MS2 .mzML query spectra against GNPS, HMDB, and MassBank spectral libraries and scoring candidate metabolite matches per LC-MS2 feature. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-matching-peak-scoring))
- **gnps-metadata-sample-mapping**: Use when running Inventa or GNPS Feature-Based Molecular Networking on an extract set and you must supply a metadata table with cleaned, up-to-date species names and organ information per sample. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-metadata-sample-mapping))
- **gnps-metadata-schema-validation**: Use when preparing to submit a dataset (e.g. MSV000083437) to MassIVE or launch a GNPS molecular networking job and the metadata table must be checked/normalized to the GNPS sample-information schema. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-metadata-schema-validation))
- **gnps-metadata-taxonomy-column-mapping**: Use when building the Inventa priority rank from a GNPS FBMN job and the metadata table must carry cleaned species/genus/family columns so LC can be computed from LOTUS taxonomic compound counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-metadata-taxonomy-column-mapping))
- **gnps-mgf-format-handling**: Use when you have mass spectrometry MS/MS spectral data in GNPS-style MGF format and need to feed it into the Mass2SMILES deep learning model for structure and functional group prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/gnps-mgf-format-handling) · 2 copies)
- **gnps-modified-cosine-fingerprint-computation**: Use when query MS/MS spectra (e.g., MassQL-filtered siderophore hits) must be converted into sample-wise similarity fingerprints to feed the NPOmix k-nearest neighbor classifier or compare spectra across paired genome–metabolome samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-modified-cosine-fingerprint-computation))
- **gnps-modified-cosine-scoring**: Use when building MS/MS testing-matrix fingerprints for NPOmix: each query spectrum needs a maximum modified cosine score against experimental spectra of each genome-matched sample. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-modified-cosine-scoring))
- **gnps-molecular-family-mapping**: Use when a metabolomics intensity matrix contains peaks grouped by tandem-MS fragmentation spectra (GNPS FBMN Molecular Families, MS2LDA Mass2Motifs) and these groupings must be turned into metabolite-set membership tables for PALS activity-level scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-molecular-family-mapping))
- **gnps-molecular-network-integration**: Use when you have computed frequent fragmentation patterns from a collection of MS/MS spectra using mineMS2, and you want to focus pattern interpretation on subsets of spectra that form meaningful network components (connected groups, cliques, or high-similarity pairs) in a GNPS molecular network. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/gnps-molecular-network-integration) · 3 copies)
- **gnps-molecular-network-interpretation**: Use when you have a GNPS molecular network with detected components (e.g. pairs or clusters of similar MS/MS spectra) and a library of mineMS2 fragmentation patterns, and you want the top patterns explaining each component. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-molecular-network-interpretation))
- **gnps-molecular-networking-annotation**: Use when you have an MGF file of tandem MS spectra plus a MZmine (&gt;2.53) quantitative feature table and need per-node spectral-library annotation scores (cosine scores) for every ion feature before k-aggregation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-molecular-networking-annotation))
- **gnps-molecular-networking-data-handling**: Use when a paired-omics linking analysis requires GNPS molecular networking output to be downloaded, extracted, and mapped to spectrum identifiers for subsequent scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-molecular-networking-data-handling))
- **gnps-molecular-networking-data-parsing**: Use when you have downloaded a GNPS archive from either GNPS1 (https://gnps.ucsd.edu) or GNPS2 (https://gnps2.org) and need to programmatically load and validate its contents (spectra.mgf, molecular_families.tsv, annotations.tsv, file_mappings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/gnps-molecular-networking-data-parsing) · 2 copies)
- **gnps-molecular-networking-job-configuration**: Use when a large collection of tandem mass spectra (e.g., ~10M MS/MS spectra across eight GNPS datasets such as MSV000079506 or MSV000080102) must be clustered into spectral families via pairwise spectral similarity before or after spectral library searches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gnps-molecular-networking-job-configuration))
- **gnps-molecular-networking-job-submission**: Use when raw MS2 data (.mzML/.mgf) has been uploaded to MassIVE and the goal is to cluster spectra into molecular families, annotate known metabolites via library matching, and propagate annotations along edge mass shifts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-molecular-networking-job-submission))
- **gnps-molecular-networking-output-parsing**: Use when preparing individual or lists of MS/MS spectra as USI-formatted .csv/.tsv entries or MZmine .mgf output to run against microbeMASST/domainMASSTs via the Fast Search API. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-molecular-networking-output-parsing))
